viśvanāthaḥ : amaratāṁ nītam amṛta-pāna-prabhāvāt maraṇa-śūnyatāṁ prāptam iti | amṛta-prabhāvo darśitaḥ | ajo brahmā grahaṁ sūryādikam iva grahatvādhikāravantam ||26||

### [8.9.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/9.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :laukika-hetu-sāmye’pi phala-prāptir bhaktimatām eva nānyeṣām ity evākhyāna-tātparyam āha—evam iti | samā deśādayo yeṣāṁ te, deśaḥ kṣīra-sindhuḥ kālas tat-tat-kṣaṇa-samūhaḥ hetur manthana-sādhanaṁ mandarādiḥ | arthaḥ samudre kṣiptaṁ vīrudhādi karma mathanaṁ matir amṛta-kāmatā | phale phala-prāptir bhaven naveti viklapavantaḥ | tatra tayor madhye sura-gaṇāḥ phalam āpuḥ | yat-pādāśrayaṇāt daityā na āpuḥ yad-āśrayaṇābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ | sa eva sevya iti prakaraṇārthaḥ ||28||

### [8.9.29](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/9.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tasmāt phalitam āha—yad iti | asavaḥ prāṇā indriyāṇi, vasu dhanam | asu-vasv-ādibhir yad dehādiṣu yujyate viniyujyate, tad asat | prāṇādīnāṁ dehādy-arthe yo viniyogaḥ so’sādhur ity arthaḥ | kutaḥ ? pṛthaktvāt śākhā-niṣecanavat | mūlāt śākhā bhinnā iti buddhyā yathā mūlaṁ vihāya śākhā niṣicyante, tathaiva bhagavantaṁ vihāya dehādyāḥ paricaryante mūḍhaiḥ | tair eva asu-vasv-ādibhir yad bhagavati kriyate asv-ādīnāṁ bhagavati yo viniyogaḥ sa sādhur ity arthaḥ | kutaḥ apṛthaktvāt | mūla-niṣecanavat | mūlāt śākhā na bhinnā iti buddhyā yathā mūlam eva sicyate tathaiva bhagavān eva paricaryate | tat-paricaraṇaṁ sarvasya brahmādi-stamba-paryantasyety arthaḥ | yad yasmāt tan mūla-niṣecanam ||29||

## Глава 10

### [8.10.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : daśame’ntarhite viṣṇau daityair yuddhe pravartite | daitya-māyābhibhūteṣu sureṣv āvirabhūd dhariḥ ||1||

### [8.10.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yayāv antardadhe ||2||

### [8.10.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : raṇo babhūva ||5||

### [8.10.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.10.10-12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bastaiś chāgaiḥ ||11||

### [8.10.13-15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vātenoddhūtair uttarair uttarīyair uṣṇīṣaiś cārcibhir arciṣmadbhiḥ sūryasya raśmibhiḥ sutarāṁ sphuradbhiḥ | dhvajinyau sene | yādasāṁ mālābhiḥ sāgarāv iva ||13-15||

### [8.10.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.10.17-18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.10.25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.25?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prasravaṇāni dāna-jalā nirjharāś ca ||25||

### [8.10.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.10.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : asyata āsyata astrāṇi cikṣepa ||28||

### [8.10.30-31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.30?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sūryo deva eka eva śatena bali-sutaiḥ niśumbha-śumbhābhyāṁ devī brahma-putrair vasiṣṭhādyaiḥ ||30-31||

### [8.10.32-34](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ: na vyākhyātam.

### [8.10.36](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.36?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bhūśuṇḍy-ādayo’stra-viśeṣāḥ ||36||

### [8.10.37](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.37?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ārohantīty ārohā bāhyās taiḥ sahitā vāhā vāhanāḥ | īṣvāso dhanuḥ tanutraṁ kavacam ||37||

### [8.10.38](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.38?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :cūrṇitāc cūrṇībhūtāt āyodhanāt saṅgrāma-sthānāt utthito reṇur nyavartata | kīdṛśāt ? asṛk-srutibhī rakta-kṣaraṇaiḥ pariplutāt siktāt | paripluta iti reṇu-viśeṣaṇam | tadā ca sūrya-paryantaṁ raktocchalanaṁ jñeyam ||38||

### [8.10.39](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.39?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prakarṣeṇa āstṛtā ācchāditāḥ ||39||

### [8.10.40](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.40?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : patitāni yāni sva-śirāṁsi tatratyair akṣibhiḥ paśyantaḥ ||40||

### [8.10.41](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.41?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vāhān airāvata-pāda-rakṣakān, ārohaṁ gajayantāram ārcchayat ivvyādha ||41||

### [8.10.43](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.43?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : durmarṣo’sahano baliḥ ||43||

### [8.10.44-45](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.44?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

viśvanāthaḥ : ṭaṅkavat tīkṣṇaiḥ śikharaiḥ saha vartamānāḥ ||46||

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.10.56](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.56?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :ibhāri-vāhaḥ siṁha-vāhaḥ | āvidhya kampá patad eva śūlaṁ vāma-hastena gṛhītvā tenaivāhanat ahan ||56||

### [8.10.57](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/10.57?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yac-cakreṇa yasya cakreṇa taṁ harim āhatya aṇḍajendraṁ garuḍam ahanat, yāvat hantuṁ pravavṛte tāvad eva acchinat | ādyo hariḥ ||57||

## Глава 11

### [8.11.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ekādaśe sa-kopoktir indro jambhādikān ahan | nāradoktyā yuddha-bhaṅge śukro daityān ajīvayat ||

### [8.11.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.11.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : naṭavad iti soktaṁ vivṛṇoti | naṭo hi bālān nibaddhākṣān kṛtvā kapaṭena jitvā tad-dhanaṁ harati, tathaiva kim asmān jigīṣasi ||4||

### [8.11.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : utsisṛpsanti divam apy ullaṅghayitum icchanti mahar-lokādiṣv apy adhikartum icchantīty arthaḥ | pūrvasmāt rasātalād apy adhaḥ pātayāmi ||5||

### [8.11.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.11.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kāla-raśanaṁ kāla-yantritaṁ paśyanty eva na tu harṣa-śokādikaṁ prāpnunvanti ||8||

### [8.11.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tatra kīrti-jayādāv ātmānaṁ svaṁ sādhanam ahaṅkāra-mauḍhyād eveti bhāvaḥ | sādhubhir jīvanto’pi yathā yūyaṁ śocyadhve, tathā vayaṁ mṛtā api na śocyāmahe iti bhāvaḥ ||9||

### [8.11.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.11.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/11.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

viśvanāthaḥ : turāṣāḍ indraḥ | agraṇīḥ sārathiḥ ||26||

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

## Глава 12

### [8.12.1-2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : māyā-maugdhyād avaidagdhyād daityānāṁ mohanena kim | iti mohayituṁ śambhum anaiṣīt svāntikaṁ hariḥ || dvādaśe mohayan śambhuṁ śapantyāḥ śambhu-yoṣitāḥ | mohinyā vibhramasyāsādhāraṇyaṁ tāv abūbudhat ||1||

### [8.12.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : somayā umayā saha ||3||

### [8.12.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : he deva-deva ! nanu tvam api deveṣu dīvyasīti tatrāha—he jagad-vyāpin ! mama jagan-madhya-vartitvān mām api tvaṁ vyāpnoṣīty arthaḥ | ato mat-sahitaṁ jagad idaṁ tava īśitavyam evety āha—he jagadīśa ! nanu, viśveśvaratvena tvam ucyase lokais tatrāha, he jagan-maya ! tvaṁ cinmayo’pi jaganmayaḥ jagan-mayatvād īśitavyo’pi īśvaraḥ | na tv aham īdṛśaḥ kintu brahmedrādivad upacārād eva viśveśvara iti bhāvaḥ | nanu tarhi māṁ tvaṁ pradhāna-svarūpaṁ brūṣe tasyaiva jagad-upādānatva-prasiddher jagad-vapuṣṭvam | tatrāha—sarveṣāṁ bhāvānāṁ vastūnāṁ tvam ātmā cetayitā ca hetuś ca ity atas tvam eveśvara iti bhāvaḥ ||4||

### [8.12.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu jagan-mayatvoktyā jagata utpatty-ādīnāṁ naśvaratvena jāḍyena ca mamāpi kiṁ tathātvaṁ brūṣe ? nahi nahīty aha—ādy-antāv iti | asya jagataḥ yau ādy-antau janma-mṛtyū yac ca madhyaṁ jīvanaṁ yad idaṁ pratyakṣaṁ vastu anyat parokṣam | aham ahaṅkārāspadam | bahir mamakārāspadaṁ ca vastu yato bhavati tad brahma cid-rūpo bhavān avyayasya brahmaṇas tavādy-antādīni na santi ||5||

### [8.12.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tavaivambhūtatve mahatām ācāra eva pramāṇam ity āha—tavaiva, na tu mama | mama tu sakāmā eveti bhāvaḥ | śreyaskāmā bhaktīcchavaḥ | ubhayatra ihāmutra ca ||6||

### [8.12.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yathā tvad-bhaktā nirāśiṣaḥ, tathā tvam api nirāśīr na tv asmad-ādir ivaiśvarya-kāmanayā sevakasyāpekṣo yatas tvaṁ viśvasmāt vilakṣaṇa-svarūpa evety āha—tvam iti | brahma cin-mayaṁ viśvaṁ tv acit evaṁ pūrṇam ity-ādi-viśeṣaṇair viśvam apūrṇaṁ sa-mṛtikaṁ sa-guṇaṁ sa-śokaṁ sukha-duḥkhātmakaṁ sa-vikāraṁ tathā na vidyate anyat yataḥ sad brahma ananyat sarva-kāraṇatvāt brahma-kāryatvam eva spaṣṭayati viśvasya udayādīnāṁ hetuḥ tad-upādhīnām ātmanāṁ jīvānām īśvaraś ca tat-phala-dātā, tarhi kiṁ rājādivat kayāpy apekṣayā sevakebhyaḥ phalaṁ dadāmi, nahi nahi, tad-apekṣatayā teṣām apekṣā yatra sa tad-apekṣas tasya bhāvas tattā tayaiva phalaṁ dadāsi, na tu svāpekṣayā | tair jīvair eva sva-sva-phala-prāpty-arthaṁ tvam apekṣyase ity arthaḥ | tvaṁ tu anapekṣaḥ sva-prayojanārthaḥ, tān nāpekṣase ||7||

### [8.12.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kiṁ ca, jīvānāṁ tvadīya-taṭa-śakti-kāryatvāt tad-upādher viśvasya tvadīya-māyā-śakti-kāryatvād idaṁ cij-jaḍātmakaṁ jagad api tvam evety āha—eka iti | sad-asat-kārya-kāraṇātmakaṁ jagat tatra dvayaṁ kāryaṁ virāṭ | advayaṁ kāraṇaṁ mahad-ādi, kim iva kṛtākṛtaṁ svarṇam iva | kṛtaṁ kuṇḍalādi akṛtaṁ kevala-svarṇam | iha virāṇ mahad-ādyoḥ kārya-kāraṇayoḥ vastunaḥ parama-kāraṇatvāt tvatto bhedo nāsti kārya-kāraṇayor abhedāvagamāt, virājo mahad-ādi-kāryatvāt mahad-āder māyā-kāryatvāt māyāyās tvac-chaktitvena tvad-rūpatvād iti bhāvaḥ | ata etad ajñānata eva tvayi ekasminn eva janair vikalpo vihitaḥ | jagat-kāraṇaṁ brahmeti māyeti ajñānam iti karmeti vividhā kalpanā kṛtā yasmān nirupādhikasya guṇātīta-svarūpasya tava guṇair eva vyatikaro vyasanaṁ vividha-rūpāvipattir iti yāvat, yac-chaktayo vadatāṁ vādināṁ vai vivāda-saṁvāda-bhuvo bhavanti | [bhā.pu. 6.4.31] iti haṁsaguhyokteḥ | ataḥ vyatikaraḥ puṁsi vyasana-vyatisaṅgayor iti medinī ||8||

### [8.12.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tad evaṁ sva-sva-maty-anusāreṇa tvām ṛṣayo bahudhā varṇayantīty āha—pareśaṁ parameśvaraṁ tvāṁ brahma vedāntino’vayanti manyante | dharmaṁ mīmāṁsakāḥ, prakṛti-puruṣayoḥ paraṁ sāṅkhyāḥ | anye vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānā kriyā yogā prahvī satyā īśānā anugrahā cety evaṁ navabhiś cic-chaktibhir yutaṁ miśritaṁ māyā-śaktes tu paraṁ pāñcarātrāḥ | mahā-puruṣaṁ pātañjalāḥ ||9||

### [8.12.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yadyapi sarva-mateṣu madhye pañcarātram eva bhagavan-mataṁ, purāṇasya kṛtasyanasya vaktā tu bhagavān svayam ity-ukteḥ, tubhyaṁ ca nārada bhṛśaṁ bhagavān vivṛddha- bhāvena sādhu parituṣṭa uvāca yogam | jñānaṁ ca bhāgavatam ātma-sa-tattva-dīpaṁ yad vāsudeva-śaraṇā vidur añjasaiva || [bhā.pu. 2.7.19] ity ukteḥ, tad eva tattvaṁ, tad eva mahā-bhaktasya śabhor api mataṁ, tad api jānīma ity abhimānino na jānantīty āśayenāha—neti | na kevalam ahaṁ na jānāmi, kintuparāyur dviparārdha-jīvī brahmā sattva-guṇena sraṣṭā api yena tvayā viracitaṁ viśvam api khalu tattvato na jānanti, kiṁ punas tvām | he īśa ! abhadraṁ rājasaṁ tāmasaṁ ca vṛttam yeṣāṁ te, taṁ tvāṁ na jānantīti kim uta vaktavyam, tena pūrvoktāḥ śāstra-kṛto’py abhimānino na jānantīti bhāvaḥ ||10||

### [8.12.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tarhi ko jānātīti cet, tatrāha—tvam eva svaṁ paraṁ ca jānāsīty āha | sa prasiddhas tvaṁ sarvam avagamaḥ avagacchan jānann eva sann avaruntse vyāpnoṣi | kiṁ tat sarvam ? samīhitaṁ sva-kṛtam amuṣya jagato sthity-ādi-bhūtānām prāṇi-mātrāṇām īhitaṁ bhava-bandhaṁ ca tasmān mokṣaṁ ca | bandha-mokṣa-prakāraṁ jānāsīty arthaḥ | vyāpti-mātre dṛṣṭāntaḥ—vāyur iti | khaṁ mahākāśaṁ sva-sthānaṁ carācarākhyaṁ deha-jātaṁ ca yathā viśati, tathaiva tvaṁ nija-dhāma-vaikuṇṭham adhyāsīna eva tad-ātmakatayā tasya sarvasyātmatayety arthaḥ ||11||

### [8.12.12-13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : abhilaṣitaṁ brūhīti cet tam āha—te guṇair bhakta-vātsalyādibhiḥ ||12-13 ||

### [8.12.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prahasyeti svasya yogeśvaratvaṁ lokeṣu prakhyāpayituṁ mohinīṁ didṛkṣase cet satyaṁ te tat sarvaṁ vyaktībhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | bhāva-gambhīraṁ mahādevenāpi duṣpraveśam ||14||

### [8.12.15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : daityānāṁ kautūhalāyeti | tādṛśānāṁ moha-janakaṁ, tad ati vismayāspadaṁ yoṣid-vapuḥ yuṣmākaṁ yogeśvarāṇāṁ tu tad akiñcit-karaṁ, na hi mṛgayuṁ mṛgo hiṁsituṁ kṣamata iti bhāvaḥ | daityānām iti tatraiva sthitāḥ sāttvikatvād devā api naiva mumuhur iti bhāvaḥ | nanu daityānāṁ tucchānāṁ kṛte ko’yaṁ te yatnaḥ ? tatrāha—paśyeti | gaty-antarābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ ||15||

### [8.12.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bhavatu, yathā yathā tad api tad-artham etāvad dūra-gamanaṁ tava na moghaṁ bhavitum arhatīty āha—tad iti | didṛkṣur ity anyathā te tatraiva viśiṣṭa-buddhir nāpayāsyaty eveti bhāvaḥ | saṅkalpa-prabhavaḥ kāmas tasyodayo yasmāt | ataḥ kāmināṁ bahu mantavyam | tava tu kāma-śatroḥ kāma udbhavayitum naiva śaknoti, tad api kautukaṁ cet paśyeti bhāvo bāhyaḥ | ābhyantaraṁ tu kāma-jetur api mahā-yogīśvarasyāpi tava tad-didṛkṣaiva kāma-sindhu-mahāvarta-niḥkṣepe hetur abhūn mama nātra doṣa iti bhāvaḥ ||16||

### [8.12.18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.18?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : lasad-dukūlena hetunā pari sarvato-bhāvena āste adarśanaṁ prāpte nitambe mekhalā yasyās tām ||18||

### [8.12.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ūrdhvam utkṣiptaṁ kandukaṁ grahītuṁ yat khalv āvartanam āvṛttiḥ, tad-anantaram udvartanam unmukhatvena vṛttiḥ, tena kampitayoḥ prakṛṣṭa-hārāṇāṁ coru-bhāraiḥ pratipadaṁ madhyataḥ madhya-deśe bhajyamānām iva calat-padam eva pravālavad aruṇaṁ tatas tato nayantīm ||19||

### [8.12.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dikṣu bhramataḥ kadācit catur-dikṣu kṣpiyamānasya kandukasya cāpalaiś cāñcalyair hetubhiḥ prodvigna-tāre āyate lole locane yasyās tām ||20||

### [8.12.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :sannahyantīṁ badhnantīm | ātmano māyayā bahiraṅga-śaktyaiva jagan mohayantīṁ, samprati jita-māyam ātmārāmam api śambhum ātma-svarūpeṇaiva mohayantīm iti bhāvaḥ ||21||

### [8.12.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : devaḥ śrī-rudraḥ | ity evaṁ-bhūtayā kanduka-līlayā māṁ kaścit sundaraḥ puruṣaḥ paśyatīti pratyāyitayā buddhyā yā īṣad-vrīḍā kandarpa-vikāra-dyotakī-kṛtaṁ yat sphuṭa-smitaṁ, tābhyām saha visṛṣṭo yaḥ kaṭākṣaḥ, tena muṣṭaś corita-yogaiśvarya-dhairya-vivekāditvāj jaḍībhūta ity arthaḥ | svayaṁ striyāḥ prekṣaṇaṁ, tayā ca pratisamīkṣaṇaṁ, tābhyāṁ vihvala ātmā mano yasya saḥ ||22||

### [8.12.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : taṁ kandukam anuvrajantyāḥ striyāḥ | laghu sūkṣmaṁ vāsaḥ | sūkṣma-śāṭikāṁ sa-sūtraṁ sūtra-baddham aharat uccikṣepa ||23||

### [8.12.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : visajjantyāṁ māṁ pāśyati na paśyatīti dyotakaiḥ kuñcita-kaṭākṣair mahādeve svāsaktim abhinayantyām ||24||

### [8.12.25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.25?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tat-padaṁ tasyāḥ sthānam ||25||

### [8.12.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vivastrā skhalad-uttarīyā nānvatiṣṭhata bhavam anulakṣīkṛtya na sthitavatī, kintu dudrāvaiva ||26||

### [8.12.29-30](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : māyayā yoga-māyayā devena hariṇā vinirmitā prakaṭīkṛtā ||29-30||

### [8.12.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ivety utprekṣā-dyotakam | vastuto bhagavataiva vinirjita ity arthaḥ ||31||

### [8.12.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śuṣmiṇo mattasya yūthapasya hastīndrasya, retaso’moghatvaṁ svarṇatvena pariṇāmāt ||32||

### [8.12.33](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.33?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : rudrasyeti | tāni kṣetrāṇi rudra-daivatāni ity atas tebhyo hema-lipsubhiḥ prathamaṁ rudraḥ prasādanīya iti bhāvaḥ ||33||

### [8.12.34](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.34?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : evaṁ tām anudhāvan haraḥ sarid-ādiṣu sannihito babhūveti mohinyās tatra tatra dhāvanaṁ tatra tatra sthānuṣīn harasya yoga-bhraṁśaṁ sākṣād darśayitum iti dhvaniḥ | tena munibhir yogārūḍhair api vijitam api sva-mano yuvatīṣu na viśvasanīyam iti śikṣaṇam anudhvaniḥ ||34||

### [8.12.35](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.35?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : skanne sampūrṇe satīty arthaḥ | mohinī-prathama-darśana-kṣaṇam ārabhyaivātmā jaḍīkṛta evāsīt, tadānīm apaśyad iti jaḍīkṛtatvenānusandadhāv ity arthaḥ | kaśmalāt mohiny-anudhāvanāt ||35||

### [8.12.36](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.36?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : atha sva-vivekotpatti-kṣaṇa eva mohinīm antarhitām ālakṣya avagataṁ sva-moha-vivekau bhagavad-adhīnāv iti māhātmyaṁ yena saḥ | ātmano hareḥ | yad vā, ātmanaḥ svasya avagata-māhātmyaḥ jñāta-yogaiśvarya-prabhāvaḥ | tat jaḍīkaraṇaṁ viveka-pradānaṁ ca adbhutaṁ na mene | tatra hetuḥ—jagad-ātmanaḥ jagan-madhya-vartinaṁ mām api mohayitum prabodhayitum ca samarthasyaivety arthaḥ ||36||

### [8.12.37](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.37?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : hanta hanta mahā-yogeśvaro’py ahaṁ viṣayāndha evābhūvam ity anutāpa-rāhityād aviklavam | svāntaryāmiṇi kā lajjety avrīḍam | ayaṁ bhāvaḥ—nāham anyena kenāpi mohituṁ śakyaḥ | mat-prabhunā tu man-mohanaṁ na dūṣaṇāvahaṁ, pratyuta bhūṣaṇāvaham eva | mamāpi mohanaṁ vinā mat-prabhor ātyantikaṁ prabhutvam eva kuta iti prabhutvātiśayo dāsasya me bhakty-utkarṣam eva puṣyatīti ||37||

### [8.12.38](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.38?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : strī-rūpayā mohinyākhya-svarūpa-bhūtayā māyayā mohito’pi svāṁ niṣṭhāṁ bhakty-uttha-dainya-mayīṁ nirahaṅkāratām ity arthaḥ ||38||

### [8.12.39](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.39?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : jagan-mohinyā mad-bahiraṅga-māyayā tu tvaṁ naiva mohito vartasa ity āha—ko nv iti | viṣaktaḥ māyika-viṣayāsaktaḥ san ko māyāṁ taret ? tvad-ṛte tvāṁ vinā tvaṁ tu māyāyām āsakto’pi māyām atara ity arthaḥ | māyāṁ viśinaṣṭi bhāvān rajas-tamo-mayān kāmādīn ||39||

### [8.12.40](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.40?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : saiva kā ? iti ced ataḥ svāṅgulyā tat-samīpa-sthāṁ durgāṁ darśayati—seyam iti | kālena kāraṇena mayā jagat sṛjatā sametā sahitā | mayā kīdṛśena ? bhāgaśo’ṁśena kalayati īkṣaṇenāṅgīkarotīti kālaḥ puruṣo rūpaṁ yasya tena ||40||

### [8.12.41](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.41?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śrīvatsāṅgenety anena praṇataḥ sa utthāpya vakṣasā saṁspṛśya āliṅgita ity arthaḥ ||41||

### [8.12.42](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.42?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śaṁsatāṁ praśaṁsatas tān munīn anādṛtya ||42||

### [8.12.43](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.43?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.12.44](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.44?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na viśata iti | kālaḥ sarvatra prabhavituṁ viśann api yatra na viśati, vedaḥ sarvaṁ jānann api yatra jñātuṁ na viśati, kāla-vedayor api yo na gamya ity arthaḥ ||44||

### [8.12.45](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.45?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.12.46](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.46?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na riṣyate na naśyati ||46||

### [8.12.47](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/12.47?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : aṅghriṁ prapannān amara-varyān sindhu-mathyaṁ sindhu-mathanodbhutam amṛtaṁ ya āśayat abhojayat | kapaṭaḥ yuvatī-veśo yasya saḥ ||47||

## Глава 13

### [8.13.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : manvantarāṇi manv-ādi-ṣaṭkavanti samāsataḥ | saptamādīni kathyante kramād atra trayodaśe ||1||

### [8.13.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ādity-ādayo devā ity anvayaḥ ||4||

### [8.13.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.13.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam

### [8.13.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam

### [8.13.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.13.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.13.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.13.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yo’sāv iti punar niveśita ity anvayaḥ ||14||

### [8.13.15-16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam

### [8.13.17-21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.13.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam

### [8.13.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam

### [8.13.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam

### [8.13.29](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : manor antaraṁ manvantaram ity arthahetu | sahasaḥ pituḥ ||29||

### [8.13.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : divaspater indrasya upahartā upakartā ||32||

### [8.13.34](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.34?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tapasvina ṛṣayaḥ ||34||

### [8.13.35](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.35?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.13.36](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/13.36?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yuga-sahasreṇa paryāyo’nto yasya saḥ |

## Глава 14

### [8.14.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : manv-ādīnāṁ tu karmāṇi ṣaṇṇāṁ yāni bhavanty atha | caturdaśe prakathyante teṣāṁ tāni pṛthak pṛthak ||

### [8.14.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : puruṣeṇa yajñādy-avatāra-rūpeṇa śāsyanta iti te tathā ||2||

### [8.14.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śāsanam āha—yajñādaya iti ||3||

### [8.14.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ṛṣy-ādīnāṁ karmāṇy āha—catur-yugānte | yato yebhyaḥ ||4||

### [8.14.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : mahīṁ dharmaṁ sañcārayanti | mahyāṁ dharmaṁ pravartayantīty arthaḥ ||5||

### [8.14.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prajā-pālā manu-putrās taṁ dharmaṁ pālayanti yāvad antaṁ manvantara-paryantam | vibhāgaśaḥ putra-pautrādi-krameṇa | ye ca devās te ca pālayanti ||6||

### [8.14.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : manv-ādi-rūpeṇa harir eva sarvaṁ karotīti jñāpayan manv-ādīnāṁ ṣaṭkam api na niyatam ity uktān uktaṁ saṅkṣepeṇāha—jñānam iti dvābhyām | siddhāḥ sanakādayaḥ | yogeśā dattātreyādayaḥ ||8||

### [8.14.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prajeśā marīcy-ādayaḥ | svarājo manu-putrāḥ | kālā yauvana-vārdhaky-ādayaḥ | abhāvāya nāśāya bhavati | pṛthag-vidhāḥ sthaulya-kārśnya-vādhirya-pālityādayo guṇā yataḥ saḥ ||9||

### [8.14.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : icchā-mātreṇaiva sarvadā sarvaṁ kartuṁ samarthasya bhagavata etāvadbhiḥ pṛthak pṛthak prayāsavadbhī rūpaiḥ kim ? iti cet, tatrāha—nāma-rūpātmikayā māyayā vimohitātmabhir ebhiḥ śāstrajñair janaiḥ stūyamāno nirūpyamāṇo’pi nyāyādi-nānā-darśanair naiva dṛśyate jñāyate ity arthaḥ | tena tasya līlaiva kīrtyate vidhitsitaṁ tv atidurgamam iti bhāvaḥ ||10||

### [8.14.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/14.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : evaṁ yatra caturdaśa-manvāntarāṇy āhuḥ | etat kalpa-vikalpasya mahā-kalpāvāntara-kalpasya naimittikasya pramāṇam ||11||

## Глава 15

### [8.15.1-2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : balir labdha-rathāśvādi kṛtād viśvajito makhāt | svargam gataḥ surā lilyur iti pañcadaśe kathā ||1||

### [8.15.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yuddhe devaiḥ parājitā śrīr yasya saḥ | hāpitas tyājitaḥ | mahātmā udāra-cittaḥ | arthānāṁ samarpaṇena ||3||

### [8.15.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : viśvajitā yajñena ||4||

### [8.15.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : hary-aśvasyendrasya ye turaṅgāṇām iva varṇā harito yeṣām te ||5||

### [8.15.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pitāmahaḥ prahlādaḥ | jalajaṁ śaṅkham ||6||

### [8.15.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : viprair arjitāḥ saṁpāditā yodhanārthā yuddhārthaka-vastūni yasya saḥ | āmantrya pṛṣṭvā ||7||

### [8.15.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : daitya-yūthapair sann indra-purīṁ yayāv iti dvitīyenānvayaḥ | paridhīn diśaḥ ||10||

### [8.15.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pravālādīnām urur bhāro yāsu tāḥ śākhā yeṣāṁ te’mara-drumā yeṣu taiḥ | yatra nandan’adi-vaneṣu nalinyaḥ sarasyaḥ santi | yāsu nalinīṣu pramadāḥ striyaḥ ||13||

### [8.15.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : purīṁ viśinaṣṭi | ākāśety-ādi | parikhā-bhūtayā parikhā-rūpayā ||14||

### [8.15.15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : gopuraṁ pura-dvāram | vibhaktāḥ prapathā rāja-mārgā yasyām tām ||15||

### [8.15.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sabhā upaveśa-sthānāni, catvarāṇy aṅganāni, rathyā upamārgāḥ, śṛṅgāṭakaiś catuṣpathaiḥ ||16||

### [8.15.17](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śīta-kāle bhaved uṣṇā uṣma-kāle suśītalāḥ | stanau sukaṭhinau yāsāṁ tāḥ śyāmāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||17||

### [8.15.18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.18?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : hema-mayebhyo jālaākṣebhyo gavākṣebhyaḥ | sura-priyā apsarasaḥ ||19||

### [8.15.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : muktā-mayair vitānaiś candrātapair maṇi-hema-mayaiḥ ketubhir dhvajaiḥ patākā-yuktābhir valabhībhir aṭṭopari-varti-gṛhaiḥ ||20||

### [8.15.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : jita-prabhā sākṣāt tad-dīpty-adhiṣṭhātrī devatāpi yayā tām | jita-grahām iti pāṭhe jita-sūryādikam ||21||

### [8.15.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ebhiḥ khalatvādibhiḥ ||22||

### [8.15.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pṛtanyayā senayā ||23||

### [8.15.25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.25?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : imam udyamaṁ baliṁ vā, āsīt abhūt ||25||

### [8.15.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prativoḍhum yuddhādibhiḥ pratikartum | yataḥ pibann ivāyaṁ baliḥ ||26||

### [8.15.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yataḥ kāraṇād oja-ādi | yataś ca oja-āder etasya samudyamaḥ ||27||

### [8.15.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śiṣyāya śraddha-bhakty-atiśayena sva-sarvasvopahārakāya balaye tejaḥ svīyam eva upabhṛtaṁ pratyupahāratvena dattam ity arthaḥ | tasmād idaṁ na bales tejaḥ, kintu brahma-teja eva | ataḥ sutarāṁ bhavadbhir durvāram iti bhāvaḥ | tena tebhyo bhṛgubhyo na nyūnā vayam api tathaiva śraddhā-bhakty-ādinā tvayā prasāditā yady abhaviṣyāma, tadā samprati tvam apy asmat-tejasā paripūrṇa imaṁ balim ajeṣya evety upālambho’nudhvanitaḥ ||28||

### [8.15.29-30](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yoddhum anenādhunā nirgacchāmi na veti ced ata āha—bhavad-vidha iti ||29||

### [8.15.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : etat-samayocitaṁ mantraṁ brūhīti ced ata āha—tasmād iti | yataḥ kālāt śatror viparyayaḥ parābhavo bhāvī, taṁ kālaṁ pratīkṣamāṇā ity arthaḥ ||31||

### [8.15.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.33](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.33?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.15.35](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.35?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ayājayan tadīyendra-pada-sthirī-bhāvārtham iti bhāvaḥ ||35||

### [8.15.37](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/15.37?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dvija-devā viprāḥ ||37||

## Глава 16

### [8.16.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ṣoḍaśe kaśyapa-praśnair aditis taṁ nyavedayat | sva-putra-duḥkha-tac-chāntyai sa provāca payo-vratam || naṣṭeṣv adṛṣṭeṣu satsu ||1||

### [8.16.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sa kaśyapaḥ ||3||

### [8.16.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dīna-vadanatvādeḥ kāraṇaṁ vikalpayan bahudhā pṛcchati—apy abhadram iti saptabhiḥ | apīti praśne | viprāṇām abhadraṁ nāgataṁ na prāptaṁ na vā dharmasya na vā lokasya | lokaṁ viśinaṣṭi—mṛtyoś chandam icchām anuvartata iti tasya ||4||

### [8.16.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : akuśalam vā dharmādeḥ | yatra gṛheṣu ayoginām karmiṇām api yogaḥ yoga-phala-prāptiḥ ||5||

### [8.16.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pṛatyutthānenāpy apūjitāḥ ||6||

### [8.16.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pheruḥ śṛgālaḥ ||7||

### [8.16.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tri-vargasya kṣetram udbhava-sthānam | tri-vargo’pi yathocitaṁ vartata ity arthaḥ ||11||

### [8.16.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bhagavatas tava | tvat-karmakād anudhyānān na riṣyati na hīyate ||12||

### [8.16.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tad api kṛpayā yady evaṁ pṛcchasi, tarhi mad-duḥkhopaśamas tvayā sukara evety āha—tavaiveti caturbhiḥ | maheśvaro bhagavān jagati sarvatra samo’pi bhaktaṁ yathā bhajat, tathaiva tvaṁ putreṣu madhye bhaktimantam indraṁ pālayitum arhasīti bhāvaḥ ||14||

### [8.16.15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na iti putra-sāhityād bahu-vacanam ||15||

### [8.16.18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.18?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kaḥ kaśyapaḥ | iveti vastuto na smayamānaḥ tasyā duḥkhenāntar-duḥkhitatvāt ||18||

### [8.16.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ātmā jīvaḥ | prakṛteḥ prākṛtād dehāt paro’nyaḥ ||19||

### [8.16.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tad api putra-snehena rudatīṁ kṛpayā punar āha—upatiṣṭhasveti puruṣaṁ vastutaḥ patim ahaṁ tu te laukika eva patir iti bhāvaḥ | bhagavantaṁ sarvajñam iti tvat-putrāṇāṁ kalyāṇopāyaṁ sa eva jānātīti bhāvaḥ | jananāmno’surasyārdanaṁ tvat-sapatnān hantum api sa eva samartha iti bhāvaḥ | janaṁ sva-bhaktaṁ baliṁ trilokīm ardiṣyati, indrārthaṁ yāciṣyate iti tam iti tu vāstavo’rthaḥ | sarva-bhūteti baler antaḥ-karaṇaṁ prerya guru-helanam api sa eva taṁ kārayiṣyate iti bhāvaḥ | vāsudeve śuddha-sattve āvirbhāvatīti vāsudevam iti bhaktyā svāntaḥ-karaṇaṁ śuddhīkṛtya tatraiva taṁ dhāyantyāṁ tvayi sa dayā-sindhur āvirbhāvitum api na vilambiṣyate iti bhāvaḥ | jagad-gurum iti tava mama baler indrasya sarva-jagato’pi gaṅgā-prādurbhāvādinā hitaṁ vidhāsyatīti bhāvaḥ ||20||

### [8.16.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : haris tvad-duḥkha-hartā ||21||

### [8.16.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : satyaḥ saṅkalpo yasmāt ||22||

### [8.16.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.25?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : payasā vrataṁ yasya saḥ payaḥ-pāyīty arthaḥ ||25||

### [8.16.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tatrādau pūrvedyuḥ kṛtyam āha—sinīvālyām amāvasyāyāṁ kroḍa-vidīrṇayā varāhotkhātayā mṛdā | yadi labhyetaitat kākākṣi-golaka-nyāyenobhayatrānvitam ||26||

### [8.16.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tatrāvāhanādau nava-mantrān āha—nama iti ||27||

### [8.16.30](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.30?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : caturviṁśatiṁ guṇān vaiśeṣikoktān rūpādīn sāṅkhyoktāni tattvāni vā jānātīti tasmai | guṇa-saṅkhyānasya sāṅkhya-śāstrasya hetave pravartakāya ||30||

### [8.16.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : guṇāvatārān praṇamati tribhiḥ | tatrādau mantroktaṁ yajña-rūpiṇaṁ viṣṇuṁ praṇamati—nama iti | tantave phala-vistārakāya | trayyāṁ vidyāyām ātmā yasyeti, tridhā baddhaḥ ity asyārtha uktaḥ | tathā ca mantraḥ— catvāri śṛṅgāṇi trayo’sya pādā dve śīrṣe sapta-hastāḥ, so’sya tridhā baddho vṛṣabho roravīti mahā-devo martyām āviveśa || iti | mārga-mudgavadālūnaḥ so’yaṁ prājñair yathā-yatham | viṣṇus tu yajña-rūpeṇa stūyate’nena tattvataḥ || iti | tathā ca yāskaḥ— catvāri śṛṅgeti vedā vā eta uktās trayo’sya pādāḥ savanāni triṇi dve śīrṣe prāyaṇīyodayanīye sapta-hastāsaḥ sapta-cchandāṁsi tri-dhā baddhas tredhā baddho mantra-brāhmaṇa-kalpair vṛṣabho roravīti roravaṇam asya savana-krameṇa ṛgbhir yajurbhiḥ sāmabhir yad enam ṛgbhiḥ śaṁsanti yajurbhir yajanti sāmabhiḥ stuvanti maho deva ity eṣa hi mahān devo yad yajño martyām āviveśety eṣa hi manuṣyān āviśati yajanāya iti ||31||

### [8.16.33](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.33?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prāṇāya sūtrātmane | yogaiśvarya-mayaṁ śarīraṁ yasya tasmai ||33||

### [8.16.34-35](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.34?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.37](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.37?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tat-pāda-padmayor āmodaṁ spṛhayanta iva devāś ca śrīś ca yam anvavartanta sa me prasīdatu ||37||

### [8.16.38](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.38?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : etair mantrair āvāhanena puraskṛtaṁ saṁmānitaṁ svaḥ-purasthīkṛtam iti vā | upasparśanam ācamanīyam ||38||

### [8.16.39](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.39?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prathamaṁ pādyādibhir arcitvā snāpayet | snāpá vastropavītābharaṇa-paridhāpanānantaraṁ punar api pādyādibhiś ca-kāreṇānyair api bahubhir upacāraiḥ | pādyādi-dāne sarvatra mantram āha—dvādaśeti ||39||

### [8.16.40](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.40?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : payasi śṛtaṁ pakvam paramānnam ||40||

### [8.16.41](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.41?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : niveditam sarvam eva dadyāt kiñcid dattvā vā bhūñjītety arthaḥ ||41||

### [8.16.42](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.42?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.43](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.43?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śeṣāṁ nirmālyaṁ, udvāso visarjanam ||43||

### [8.16.44-45](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.44?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dvau avarau yeṣām asāmarthey dvāv api bhojayet | sabhājitaiḥ srak-tāmbūlādinā pūjitais tair dattājñaḥ san seṣṭaḥ bandhu-sahitaḥ, śvo-bhūte prabhāte sati ||44-45||

### [8.16.46-47](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.46?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.48](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.48?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śukla-trayodaśrīīti prathamānto dvitīyāntaś ca kvācitkaḥ pāṭhaḥ | svapnaṁ śayanam ||48||

### [8.16.50](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.50?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pañcakaiḥ pañcāmṛtaiḥ ||50||

### [8.16.58](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.58?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.16.60](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.60?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sarva-yajñākhyo’yaṁ yajña ity anenaikenaiva sarva-yajñāḥ kṛtā bhavantīty arthaḥ | evam idaṁ sarva-vratam tapaḥ-sāram iti asya sarva-tapastvād iti bhāvaḥ | tena yajña-sāra-vrata-sāra-dāna-sāra-śabdenāpīdaṁ vācyam iti dyotitam | īśvara-tarpaṇaṁ cet ca-kārād īśvara-tarpaṇo’yaṁ yajñaḥ īśvara-tarpaṇaṁ vratam ity evaṁ ca vācyam ||60||

### [8.16.61](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/16.61?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : īśvara-tarpaṇaṁ vinā sarvam eva viphalam iti dig-darśanenāha ta eveti | anye niyamādyā eva na syuḥ | yeneti napuṁsakam anapuṁsakenety-ādi naikatvam ||61||

## Глава 17

### [8.17.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vratiny evāditir viṣṇuṁ paśyantī tuṣṭuve sa tām | āśvāsyābhūt suto brahma stutaḥ saptadaśe prabhuḥ ||1||

### [8.17.2-3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : manasā pragṛhya pragraha-rūpeṇa manasā vaśīkṛtyety arthaḥ | mano-raśmir buddhi-sūta iti pūrvokteḥ | ekāgrayā ekāgrīkṛtayā saubhāgyā manaś ca vaśīkṛtaṁ vāsudeve samyag arpá ||2-3||

### [8.17.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.17.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : udvīkṣatī utkarṣeṇa tathā vīkṣyamāṇo yathā cakṣuṣā taṁ pibatīvotprekṣate ity arthaḥ | ramā-patiṁ tat-putrebhyaḥ sarva-sampat-pradātāraṁ yajña-patiṁ yajña-sārākhya-vratād āvirbhavantaṁ jagat-patim avatīrya jagad-uddhartāram ||7||

### [8.17.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yajñeśa he vrata-yajña-phala-prada ! yajña-puruṣa he majjanmāvadhi-yajanīya-puruṣa ! acyuta ! he sva-bhakta-śakrādi-cyuti-vināśin ! tīrtha-pāda ! he gaṅgotpādayiṣyac-caraṇa-padma ! tīrtha-śravaḥ ! he jagat-pāvanī-bhaviṣyad-bali-cchalanādi-kīrte ! he karṇānandi-bhaviṣyad-upendra-trivikramādi-nāman ! he vipanna-mad-vidha-jana-duḥkha-praśamakāvatārety eva bhāvārtha-sūcaka-sambodhana-padāny adityā mukhāt svayam eva niḥsṛtānīti jñeyam ||8||

### [8.17.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tavaiśvaryam apāraṁ jñātvaivāha—tvām eva prapadye ity āha—viśvāyeti | bhūmne mahate namaḥ | sveṣu bhakteṣu tiṣṭhatīti tasmai śaśvad upabṛṁhito yaḥ pūrṇo bodhaḥ, tena vyāpāditaṁ nitya-nirastam ātmani tamo māyā-lakṣaṇaṁ yena tasmai ||9||

### [8.17.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yad vāñchitaṁ tu tvaṁ pūrayiṣyasy eveti kaimutya-nyāyenāha—āyur iti | dyaur bhūrasā iti samāsenāsamāsena ca pāṭha-dvayam | yoga-guṇā aṇimādyāḥ ||10||

### [8.17.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kṣetra-jño’ntaryāmī ||11||

### [8.17.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tasyāḥ bhakteḥ sakāmatvaṁ vyañjayān nāha yat sapatnair iti | sva-patnaiś cyāvitānāṁ devānāṁ yac cira-kāṅkṣitaṁ tad eva bhavatyāś cira-kāṅkṣitaṁ mayā jñātam ||12||

### [8.17.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : putraiḥ saha upa ādhikyena āsitum sva-rājye upaviṣṭum icchasi | yad vā, taiḥ sahaiva mām upāsituṁ, na tv ekākinīty arthaḥ ||13||

### [8.17.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : āsādya sva-sthānaṁ prāpya ||14||

### [8.17.15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.17.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : anukūlair īśvaraiḥ samarthaiś ca viprai rakṣitāḥ ||16||

### [8.17.17](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : gantuṁ bhavituṁ phalasya hetukatvāt uttama-kāraṇatvam||17||

### [8.17.18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.18?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : mārīcasya kaśyapasya tapaḥsu adhiṣṭhāya sthitaḥ san ||18||

### [8.17.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.17.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.17.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kṛta-kṛtyavat kṛtārtham eva ||21||

### [8.17.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : avitathekṣaṇaḥ avyartha-jñānaḥ ||22||

### [8.17.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vīryaṁ bhagavad-rūpaṁ tejaḥ | samāhita-manā bhagavat-svarūpaikāgra-cittaḥ | anilo yathā saṅgharṣeṇa dārustham agnim eva mūrtaṁ dāru-garbhe prakaṭīkaroti tathaiva kaśyapo’ntaḥ-karaṇasthaṁ bhagavantam eva sva-deha-saṅgena tadīya-garbha-sthirīcakārety arthaḥ | yathaivāgnir anila-dārv-aṁśo na bhavati, tathaiva bhagavad-deho’pi kaśyapādity-aṁśo na bhavatīti śukra-śoṇita-sambandho nirastaḥ ||23||

### [8.17.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dhiṣṭhitam adhiṣṭhitam | guhya-nāmabhiḥ samīḍe iti stuti-miṣeṇa bhakti-vaśyasya bhagavato nāma-saṅkīrtana-lakṣaṇāṁ bhaktim eva cakārety arthaḥ ||24||

### [8.17.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : trayo lokā nābhau yasya tasmai | trayāṇām ūrdhvādho-madhya-varti-lokānāṁ pṛṣṭha-rūpa-vaikuṇṭha-sthitatvāt tripṛṣṭhāya | śipi-śabdena paśavo jīvāḥ, teṣv antaryāmitvena praviṣṭāya ||26||

### [8.17.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : gabhīraṁ nady-ādi-srotaḥ kartṛ yathā svāntaṁ patitaṁ tṛṇādikam ākarṣati ||27||

### [8.17.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/17.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sañcariṣṇur āvirbhaviṣṇuḥ san | prajāpatīnāṁ parāyaṇaṁ paramāśrayaḥ ||28||

## Глава 18

### [8.18.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : aṣṭādaśe harir bhūtvā vāmano brahma-sūtra-bhṛt | baler yajñaṁ gatas tena varān vṛṇv iti bhāṣitaḥ || amṛta-bhūḥ prākṛtānām iva na mṛtā na nāśavatī bhūr utpattir yasya saḥ | janma-karma ca me divyam [gītā 4.9] ity ukteḥ | śaṅkha-gadābja-cakra ity arśa-ādy-aj-antam | avisarga-pāṭhe sa cāsau piśaṅga-vāsāś ca ||1||

### [8.18.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śyāmaś cāsāv avadātaś cinmayatvena śuddhaś ceti saḥ |jhaṣa-rājo makaraḥ | valayair aṅgadaiś ca sahollasanti kirīṭādīni yasya saḥ ||2||

### [8.18.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śrīmatyā vana-mālayā virājitaḥ ||3||

### [8.18.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : agni-jihvā devāḥ | nagāḥ parvatāḥ ||4||

### [8.18.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tat-prādurbhāva-samayam āha—śroṇāyām iti | bhādra-śukla-dvādaśī śravaṇa-dvādaśīti prasiddhā, tasyām | tatrāpi śroṇāyāṁ śravaṇa-sthe candra ity arthaḥ | tatrāpi śravaṇasya prathamāṁśe’bhijiti nakṣatre | tac ca śrutyā darśitam—abhijin-nāma nakṣatram upariṣṭād aṣāḍhāyāḥ caraṇāravindāyāḥ adhastāditi | jyotiṣeṇa ca | uttarāṣāḍhāśeṣārdhāt caraṇāravindādau liptikā-catuṣke ca abhijit, tat-sthe khecare vijñeya rohiṇī-viddheti | muhūrte sva-janmocita-śubha-muhūrta ity arthaḥ | yad vā, abhi sarvato-bhāvena jit jayo yataḥ, tasmin muhūrte parama-śubha-lagna ity arthaḥ | jaya-lakṣaṇam evāha—tārāgrahā guru-śukrādayaḥ, te ādyā yeṣāṁ te sūryādayo’pi sarve taj-janma dakṣiṇam udāraṁ cakruḥ iti śrī-svāmi-caraṇaiḥ ||5||

### [8.18.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ahani madhyandina-gataḥ savitā yadābhūt, tadā janmeti śeṣaḥ | yasyāṁ dvādaśyāṁ janma viduḥ sā vijayā proktā ||6||

### [8.18.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : śaṅkhādayo nedur iti śeṣaḥ ||7||

### [8.18.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : devā manava ity-ādīnāṁ kusumaiḥ samavākirann iti tṛtīyenānvayaḥ ||8||

### [8.18.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vibhūṣaṇāyudhaiḥ saha yad vapur bhāti, vartamāna-nirdeśena vapuṣo nityatvaṁ vyañjitam | kiṁ ca, yad avyaktaṁ ca cit-svarūpaṁ ca, tad eva vyaktaṁ kṛpayaiva vyaktīkṛtam | adhārayat, na tv abhūta-caram eva taj jagrāhety arthaḥ | pitroḥ sukhārtham iti bhāvaḥ | tenaiva, na tu māyikena kenacid ity arthaḥ | divyā durgamāḥ satyā gatayaś ceṣṭā yasya tathā-bhūto mahā-yogeśvaro naṭaḥ sa hi sva-svarūpataḥ pṛthag-bhūtaṁ svarūpaṁ kurvann apy apṛthag-bhūtam eva karoti | tathaiva haris tena cinmaya-vapuṣaiva vapur babhūvety arthaḥ ||12||

### [8.18.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : karmāṇi cūḍopanayanādīni ||13||

### [8.18.14-16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.18.17](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pātrikāṁ bhikṣā-pātrīm ||17||

### [8.18.18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.18?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : brahma-varcasena brahma-tejasā saṁbhāvitaḥ | kārita-sambhāvanaḥ | brahmacāritven a pratyāyita ity arthaḥ | atikramyārocata | māriṣaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ ||18||

### [8.18.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bhṛgūṇām upakalpitaiḥ bhṛgubhiḥ sampāditaiḥ | akhilaiḥ sārair viveka-dhairya-pāṇḍityādibhiḥ saṁbhṛtaḥ pūrṇaḥ | bhāreṇa kṛpaiśvaryādi garimṇā gāṁ pṛthvīṁ samyaṅ-namaskāraṁ kārayan ||20||

### [8.18.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.18.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ta ṛtvig-ādayaḥ evam vyatarkayann iti śeṣaḥ ||22||

### [8.18.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ittham iti yathā ṛtvig-ādayo vyatarkayan itthaṁ bhṛguṣu bhṛgubhir api vitarkyamāṇaḥ | hayamedha-bāṭam aśvamedha-maṇḍapam ||23||

### [8.18.24-25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vītaṁ yuktaṁ, upavītavad dhṛtam ajinam evottaram uttarīyaṁ yasya tam | māyayā svarūpeṇaiva māṇavakaṁ bālam | svarūpa-bhūtayā nitya-śaktyā māyākhyayā yutaḥ iti śruteḥ | pratyagṛhṇan yathā-vidhi nati-vinayārghyādi-dānena sammānayāmāsuḥ | pratigrahaḥ svīkaraṇe sainya-pṛṣṭhe patadgrahe | dvijebhyo vidhivad deye iti medinī ||24-25||

### [8.18.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.18.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : mukta-saṅgānām ātmārāmāṇāṁ mano ramayatīti tam ||27||

### [8.18.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : candra-maulir lalāṭe dhṛta-candro’pi mūrdhnā candrasyāpy upari dadhāra iti bhāvaḥ ||28||

### [8.18.29](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tvā tvāṁ vapur-dharaṁ mūrtimat-tapa eva manye, sākṣān mat-pratyakṣībhūtam ||29||

### [8.18.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tvac-caraṇāv anejanair vārbhiḥ | padaiś caraṇa-cihnair iyaṁ bhūḥ punītā pavitrīkṛtā ārṣaḥ prayogaḥ ||31||

### [8.18.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/18.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

## Глава 19

### [8.19.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pada-traya-mitāṁ bhūmiṁ viṣṇunā prārthaitaṁ balim | ditsantam asmai śukras tu nyayyaitasīd ūnaviṁśake ||1||

### [8.19.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dātuḥ stutiṁ svalpa-yācñāṁ santoṣa-vyañjanāṁ dhṛtim | bhikṣūn bahutaraṁ lipsūn śikṣayann āha vāmanaḥ | vaca iti ṣoḍaśabhiḥ yasya tava aihike dharme bhṛgavaḥ pramāṇam | sāmparāye pāralaukike pitāmahaḥ prahlādaḥ ||2||

### [8.19.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : niḥsattvasya lakṣaṇaṁ—pratyākhyātā niḥsattva-viśeṣasya kṛpaṇasya lakṣaṇaṁ prathamaṁ pratiśrutya yo’dātā | vā-śabdāt pratyākhyātā ||3||

### [8.19.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tīrthe dānāvasare arthino brāhmaṇādi-yācakenārthitā nṛpā dāna-parāṅmukhā yudhi yuddhāvasare arthinā kṣatriyādinā arthitā yuddha-parāṅmukhā ye amanasvinaḥ anudāra-cittā nṛpās te yuṣmat-kule na santīty arthaḥ | yeṣāṁ yaśasā ||4||

### [8.19.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yato yatra kule ||5||

### [8.19.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.19.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yatra yatrāhaṁ yāsyāmi | tatraivāsau mām na tyakṣyatīty arthaḥ | parāgdṛśaḥ parān śatrūn añcantyaḥ prāpnuvantyo dṛśo dṛṣṭayo yasya tasya, pakṣe bahir-darśinaḥ ||9||

### [8.19.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :ato hṛdaye sthitaṁ māṁ na jñāsyatīty evaṁ niścitya śvāsānile’ntarhitaḥ sūkṣmo deho yasya | vivignam ati-kampitaṁ ceto yasya | akampita-citta iti vāstavo’rthaḥ ||10||

### [8.19.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.19.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yato nāvartate pumān iti mad-bhayād eva mṛta ity arthaḥ | bauddhānāṁ mate maraṇasyaiva muktitvam | tad eva tasya matam | vastutas tu yataḥ pumāṁs tad-bhakto nāvartate, tat svīya-dhāmaiva gata ity arthaḥ ||12||

### [8.19.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vairānubandhaḥ khalv etāvān eva yāvān hiraṇyakaśipoḥ āmṛtyoḥ mṛtyu-paryanto viṣṇu-paryantaś ca mahā-śauryotsāha-hetuka iti bhāvaḥ | iha saṁsāre dehinām anyeṣāṁ tu manyur ajñāna-prabhavo moha-hetukaḥ krodha-viśeṣa eva tathā ahaṁ-mānaḥ ahaṁ śūra iti mano’bhimāna-mātraṁ śauryābhāve’py ātmani śaurya-mananaṁ tena upabṛṁhito vistāritaḥ vairānubandhas tv ekasya hiraṇyakaśipor evātra jagati dṛṣṭe iti bhāvaḥ ||13||

### [8.19.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tava pitā virocanas tu prahlādasya putraḥ tad vidvān vairānubandhaṁ jānann api dvija-liṅgebhyo dvija-veśa-dhāribhyo devebhyaḥ svam āyur adāt | yato dvija-vatsalaḥ | teṣāṁ vairitva-jñāne’pi dvija-veśa-dhāritva eva prītimān ||14||

### [8.19.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : padāni trīṇīti | tribhiḥ kramair ity upariṣṭād uktes trīn pāda-vikṣepān vyāpya yā mahī tām īṣaṇ-mātrīm iti tāvatyaiva mahyā mama parṇa-śālā bhaviṣyati | vṛttis tv ājagarī mama bhūyasy eveti bhāvo baliṁ bodhayitum iṣṭaḥ, mama padā sasmitānīti trivikrama-padābhiprāyeṇa na cānṛta-vādaḥ, tatrāpi sva-padānapagamāt ||16||

### [8.19.17](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yāvata arthaḥ prayojanaṁ tāvata eva pratigraho yasya saḥ | tāvata eveti pada-dvayasya vṛttāv antarbhāvaḥ | pakṣe—yāvantas tavārthās teṣāṁ sarveṣām eva pratigraho yasya sa mal-lakṣaṇo’yaṁ vidvān na enaḥ kaṣṭaṁ prāpnoti ||17||

### [8.19.18](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.18?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : brāhmaṇa-dāyāda ! he vipra-sūno ! brahmaṇyatvāt brāhmaṇebhyo dāyam ā samyaktayā dadātīti vastv-arthaḥ sarasvatī-prayuktaḥ, kintu tvaṁ bālaḥ yathānyo bāliśa-matis tathaiva svārthaṁ prati tvam abudhaḥ | vastuto mahābudho bhavann apīti bhāvaḥ | vastv-arthas tu tvaṁ bhakta-vatasalatvād bhaktārtham eva buddhyase | svārthaṁ prati tu na buddhyase, iva ity abudhaḥ | paripūrṇasya tava bhakta-prayojana-vyatirekeṇa sva-prayojanābhāvād iti ||18||

### [8.19.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yo bhavān iti śeṣaḥ | buddhimān sann api dvīpa-dāyinam ||19||

### [8.19.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.19.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : santuṣṭaṁ mām etāvataiva dānena pūraya | na hy asantuṣṭaṁ tvam api pūrayitum samartho’sīty āha yāvanta iti ||21||

### [8.19.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :nava-varṣa-sametenāpi dvīpa-varāṇām icchayā ||22||

### [8.19.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.19.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ajitātmā ajitendriyaḥ | upasāditaiḥ prāptaiḥ ||24||

### [8.19.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : āśuśukṣaṇir agniḥ ||26||

### [8.19.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : siddho’haṁ kṛtārtha ity etāvataiva sarvasvāpahāra-siddhir iti gūḍho’bhiprāyaḥ | yāvat yat-pramāṇakaṁ vittaṁ prayojanakaṁ bhavet tāvad eva grāhyam ity arthaḥ ||27||

### [8.19.29](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vidāṁ vara iti vāmanasya parokṣaṁ prāhety arthaḥ ||29||

### [8.19.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : upagataḥ prāptaḥ ||31||

### [8.19.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : hariḥ sarvasvaṁ hariṣyatīty arthaḥ | manaḥ-paryantaṁ ca iti vāstavaḥ ||32||

### [8.19.33](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.33?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :nanu mayā pada-trayam eva pratiśrutaṁ, nādhikam ? tatrāha—tribhir iti | kramaiḥ pāda-vinyāsaiḥ, kramatām iti cet, tatrāha—sarvasvam iti ||33||

### [8.19.34](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.34?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pratiśrutaṁ kathaṁ na saṁpādanīyam ? iti cet, tatra sarvasve datte’pi tad evaṁ pratiśrutaṁ na sampatsyate, ity āha dvābhyām | kramataḥ kramamānasya tārtīyasya tṛtīyasya | yad vā, tārtīyasya tṛtīya-sambandhinaḥ tṛtīya-pada-kramasya vastunaḥ kuto heto gatiḥ prāptis te bhaviṣyatīty arthaḥ ||34||

### [8.19.35](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.35?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : niṣṭhāṁ nitarāṁ sthitiṁ pratipādayitum pratipādane ity arthaḥ | tena sarvasva-dāne’pi narakasyāvaśyakatvaṁ ced varaṁ sarvasvādānam eva bhadram aihika-bhoga-siddhy-artham iti bhāvaḥ ||35||

### [8.19.36](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.36?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tad api yathā-śakti pratiśrutaṁ kathaṁ na dāsyāmi ? iti cet, tatrāha—neti | tapaś cittaikāgryam ||36||

### [8.19.37](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.37?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dānādāv api śāstrīyā vyavasthā vartate ity āha—dṛśhāyeti ||37||

### [8.19.38](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.38?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu deya-dravya-sattve’py anyan me nāstīty anṛta-vacanaṁ vinā mama katham adānam upapadyatām ? tatrāha sārdhaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ | atrāpi satyānṛta-vyavasthāyām om ity aṅgīkāreṇa yat proktaṁ, tat satyaṁ neti, yad āha tad evānṛtam | om iti satyaṁ nety anṛtam iti śruteḥ ||38||

### [8.19.39](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.39?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tat tu satyam īṣad anṛtaṁ vinā na siddhyatīty āśayenāha—satyam iti | ātma-vṛkṣasya deha-rūpa-vṛkṣasya satyaṁ puṣpa-phalaṁ vidyāt | gīyate iti śruti-kartṛkaṁ gānam ity arthaḥ | tat vṛkṣe’jīvati na syāt, tasmād vṛkṣo yena jīvati, tatra prayatanīyam iti bhāvaḥ | tad evāha—anṛtaṁ tena sarvathaivānṛtābhāve deho na jīvatīty arthaḥ ||39||

### [8.19.40](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.40?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :tat tasmāt yathā vṛkṣa utpāṭita-mūlaḥ śuṣyati, acirād udvartate vartanād udgacchati naṣṭo bhavati ca, evam eva naṣṭānṛtaḥ sarvathaiva anṛta-rahito dehaḥ śuṣyed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ— om iti satyaṁ nety anṛtaṁ tad etat puṣpaṁ phalaṁ vāco yat satyaṁ sa heśvaro yaśasvī kalyāṇa-kīrtir bhavitā | puṣpaṁ hi phalaṁ vācaḥ satyaṁ vadaty athaitan mūlam vāco yad anṛtaṁ tad yathā vṛkṣa āvirmūlaḥ śuṣyati, sa udvartata, evam evānṛtaṁ vadann āvirmūlam ātmānaṁ karoti, sa śuṣyati, sa udvartate, tasmād anṛtaṁ na vaded dayeta tv etenaiti | vāca iti vāg-upalakṣitasya dehasyety arthaḥ | anṛtaṁ vadann iti prakaṭīkṛtyeti śeṣaḥ | mūlaṁ yathā āviṣkṛtam eva śuṣyati, na tu guptam | tasmāt iti tasmād dhetor api anṛtaṁ na vadet, kintu dayeta tv etena iti etena anena tu anṛtena dayeta saṅkaṭeṣv ātmānaṁ rakṣed iti śruty-arthaḥ ||40||

### [8.19.41](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.41?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sarvathā satya-vacanena deho na nirvahed iti sphuṭī-kartuṁ satyasya doṣān anṛtasya guṇān āha—parāg iti dvābhyām | yad om ity akṣaraṁ tat parāk dūre’rthaṁ gṛhītva’ñcatīti parāk riktam iti śruti-padasya vyākhyānam apūrṇaṁ vai iti | tat tasmād arthine yat kiñcid om iti dāsyāmīti brūyāt, tenaiva ricyeta, na tu sarveṇaiva vittena nyūno bhavati | bhikṣave sarvam oṁ kurvan dāsyāmīty aṅgīkurvann ātmane ātmārthaḥ kāmena bhogena nālaṁ paryāpto na bhavati | tasya bhogo na siddhyatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ— parāg vā etad riktam akṣaraṁ yad etad om iti tad yat kiṁ ca, om ity āhātraivāsmai tad ricyate sa yat sa sarvam oṁ kuryāt ricyād ātmānaṁ sa kāmebhyo nālaṁ syātiti ||41||

### [8.19.42](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.42?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : neti yad anṛtaṁ vaca etat pūrṇam artha-vyayābhāvāt | abhyātmaṁ ca ātmano’bhimukham anyasyārtham ānayatīty abhyātmam | yo hi nityaṁ mama kiñcid api nāsti sīdāmīti brūte, sa hi tenānṛtena pareṣām arthān ākarṣatīti prasiddham | nanu tarhīdam anṛtam amṛtam ivātiśayena sevyam astu nety āha—sarvam iti | tathā ca śrutiḥ— athaitat pūrṇam abhyātmaṁ yan neti sa yat sarvaṁ neti brūyāt, pāpikāsya kīrtir jāyeta, sainaṁ tatraiva hanyāt || iti ||42||

### [8.19.43](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/19.43?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ato vṛtti-saṅkaṭādiṣv anṛtaṁ na doṣāyety upasaṁharati | strīṣu protsāhanena vaśīkāre, narmaṇi yatra kvāpi parihāse, vivāhe varādi-stutau, gavāṁ brāhmaṇānāṁ ca hitārthe, hiṁsāyāṁ kasyacit prāptāyām | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ—varṇināṁ hi vadho yatra tatra sākṣy anṛtaṁ vadet iti | tathā ca śrutiḥ—tasmāt kāla eva dadyāt kāle na dadyāt tat satyānṛte mithunī-karoti iti ||43||

## Глава 20

### [8.20.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : viṁśe jñātvārthinaṁ viṣṇum avajñāya guror vacaḥ dadau harṣād baliḥ so’pi prāptyā harṣād ivaidhata || kṣaṇaṁ tūṣṇīm iti bhagavad-icchā-prātikūlye kuto guror gurutvam ato’syājñā-laṅghane na doṣa iti niścikāyeti bhāvaḥ ||1||

### [8.20.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : arthādikaṁ na bādheteti tena viprasyaitādṛśa-prlambanād dharmaṁ bādhetaiva | bhaktaṁ tu bhajanīyasya bhagavato jñātasyāpi pralambhanād bādheta tamam iti bhāvaḥ ||2||

### [8.20.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prāhlādir iti prahlāda-pautrasya mama bhagavad-ānukūlyam eva sva-dharma iti gūḍho’bhiprāyaḥ | tena prahlādenāsyaṇtaḥkaraṇe kṛpayā bhakti-bījaṁ pūrvam uptam eva, samprati tu śrī-vāmana-deva-kṛpayā prāptena premṇā siddha evābhūt | yad uktaṁ—kṛpā-siddho yajña-patnī vairocani-śukādayaḥ iti ||3||

### [8.20.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : adhanyād dāridryāt ||5||

### [8.20.6](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : yad yad dhanādikaṁ kartṛ, saṁparetaṁ mṛtaṁ hāsyati tyakṣyaty eva | tena tvad-ājñayā kiñcin-mātreṇa dattena vipraś cen na tuṣyati, tarhi tasya tāvan-mātrasya tyāge kiṁ-nimittaṁ phalam ? ataḥ sarvam eva vittam etat-prīty-arthaṁ mayā dāsyata eveti bhāvaḥ | atra sveṣṭa-devaṁ viṣṇuṁ jñātvāpi pravṛddha-bhakty-uttha-sambhrama-praṇati-stuty-ādy-akaraṇaṁ śukrācāryasyāsurāṇāṁ ca duḥkhābhāvārtham eva jñeyam | ata eva vipra-śabda-prayogo bhāva-gopanārtha eva jñeyaḥ ||6||

### [8.20.7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.7?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tvaṁ sarvasvam eva ditsasīty atrārthe pūrva ācāraḥ pradarśyatām iti cet, tatrāha—śreya iti | bhūtānām api śreyaḥ, kim uta sva-gṛham āgatasya viṣṇoḥ ? asubhir ahantāpsadair api | dharādiṣu mamatāspadeṣu ko vikalpaḥ, dāsye na vā dāsye ity-ādy-ātmakaḥ ? api tu naiva vikalpaḥ, kintu dāsyāmy eveti niścaya eveti bhāvaḥ ||7||

### [8.20.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sva-kṛtyādikam anapekṣya naśvara-bhogārtham eva na dātavyam iti mataṁ tu nāsmākam abhimatam ity āha—yair iti | yuddhe’nivṛttibhiḥ | iyaṁ bhūḥ teṣāṁ lokān aihikān pāratrikāṁś ca kālo’grasīt nāśayāñcakāra, lokān lokottha-bhogān iti vā | na tu bhuvi tair adhigataṁ prāptaṁ yaśo’grasīt | ataḥ kīrtir eva sādhyā nānyad iti bhāvo’pi śukrādy-anurodhenaiva jñāpitaḥ | vastutas tu tasya kīrtādy-apekṣāpi manasi nāsti śuddha-bhaktatvād iti bhāvaḥ ||8||

### [8.20.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kiṁ ca, yuddhe nivṛtti-rahitebhyo’pi dāne nivṛtti-rahitāḥ adhika-yaśasvina ity āha—sulabhā iti tīrthe sampradāne | atas tanu-tyāgād api dhana-tyāgo duṣkaraḥ, sa eva mayā kartavya iti bhāvaḥ ||9||

### [8.20.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu tarhi tava dāridryaṁ bhaviṣyati ? iti, tatrāha—manasvino dāna-vīrasya śobhanam etad eva yad arthināṁ yācakānāṁ yādṛśa-tādṛśānām api kāmopanayane kāma-pūraṇena durgatir dāridryam | daridro durgatāv iti durgati-dāridryayos tulyathārthatvāt bhavādṛśānāṁ tu kāmopanayena durgatiḥ śobhanam iti kiṁ punar vaktavyam ity arthaḥ ||10||

### [8.20.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu nāyaṁ baṭuḥ, kintu viṣṇus tava śatrur ity uktaṁ, tarhi sutarām eva dāsyāmi ity āha—yajantīti | paraḥ śatrur vāstu ||11||

### [8.20.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bhītam iti viṣṇutve’pi brāhmaṇa-tanutva-hetukaṁ bhayam asyāvaśyambhāvīti bhāvaḥ ||12||

### [8.20.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : eṣa vai niścitam uttama-śloka eva yad yasmāt sva-yaśo na jihāsati ato bhūmir na dātavyeti mayokte yācñā-bhaṅga-parābhavam asahamānenānena, tarhi yuddhaṁ dehīti prārthite com ity uktavati sati yuddhe māṁ hatvā enāṁ bhūmiṁ hared eva | nanu mahā-vīrāt tvatto’sya parābhavo’pi sambhaved iti cet, tatrāha—śayīteti | mayā nihato yuddhe śayīteti kākuḥ | naiva śayīta viṣṇor avadhyatvād iti bhāvaḥ | yad vā, nety asyāvṛttyā asmad-arthe niṣedhārthe ca vartanād vināpi kākvā saṅgatiḥ ||13||

### [8.20.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : aśraddhā sañjātāsyety aśraddhitam | daivena bhagavat-prema-sukha-pratikūla-prācīnāparādhotthenābhāgyena prerita-buddhiḥ satya-pratijñam uttama-manaskam ||14||

### [8.20.15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : paṇḍita-mānī paṇḍitānāṁ mānanīyaḥ | na vidyate jño yasmāt saḥ | asmad-upekṣayā asmat-kartṛkayā upekṣayāpi stabdhaḥ | bhayābhāvād anamraḥ | mac-chāsanam atikramya gacchasi viṣṇum iti bhāvaḥ | tasmāt na cirād api śriyo bhraśyase | bhagavad-dattāṁ nityāṁ sampadaṁ prāpsyasīty evaṁ vāstavo’rthaḥ ||15||

### [8.20.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : etāṁ bhūmim udaka-dāna-pūrvakam ||16||

### [8.20.17](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.17?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :parama-lajjāvaty asūryampaśyāpi rājñī bhartur bhakti-niṣṭhām avagamyānandāśru-stimitā harṣotthaṁ cāpalyaṁ nihnotum aśaknuvatī dāsīr apy anapekṣyamāṇā kalasaṁ vahantī bahir niścakrāmety āha—vindhyāvalir iti | jālakaṁ mallikādy-apakva-koraka iti kecit | sa-puṣpātikomala-phalam ity anye | kṣārako jālakaṁ klība ity amaraḥ | jālakaṁ muktābharaṇa-viśeṣa iti svāmi-caraṇāḥ | avanejanīnām apāṁ kalasaṁ bhṛtaṁ pūrṇam ||17||

### [8.20.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tat tasya karma ārjavam akāuṭilyaṁ ca gṛṇantaḥ stuvantaḥ ||19||

### [8.20.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : avardhata vāñchātaḥ partigṛhyatām iti balinā pūrvam uktatvāt | guṇa-trayaṁ māyā tat-kāryaṁ ca ātmani sarvādhiṣṭhānatvāt svasminn eva yasya tat | ata eva bhūḥ kham ity-ādayo yad yasmin āsata sthitavantaḥ ||21||

### [8.20.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : guṇānām ātmake uttamādhiṣṭhātari bhūtāni ca indriyāṇi ca, arthāḥ śabdādayaś ca, āśayo’ntaḥkaraṇāni ca, jīvāṁś ca, tair yuktam ||22||

### [8.20.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :tad eva prapañcayati—rasām iti | pādonaiḥ saptabhiḥ | rasāṁ bhū-talaṁ mahīṁ bhūtaloparisthān nagara-gṛhāṭṭa-vṛkṣādīn ity arthaḥ | mahīdhrān tad-upari-gatān parvatān ity arthaḥ | patattriṇas tad-upari-carān pakṣiṇaḥ | mārutaṁ gaṇaṁ vāyu-saṅgham | indrasya seneva senā yasya saḥ ||23||

### [8.20.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ātma-mukhyān bali-pradhānān asurān | bhū-tala-lakṣito’pi baliḥ sveṣāṁ taj-jaghanādhiṣṭānatvāt jaghana evaikṣata ity arthaḥ | evam agre’pi jñeyam ||24||

### [8.20.25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.25?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ṛtaṁ priya-vākyaṁ samastān rephān rephopalakṣitān a-kārādi-sarva-varṇān ||25||

### [8.20.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dyauś ca dyāṁ ca ||26||

### [8.20.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : rase rasane | pakṣmāsu pakṣmonmīlana-nimīlanayoḥ ||27||

### [8.20.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : retasā retasi | kramaṇeṣu pāda-nyāseṣu | oṣadhi-jātīś ca ||28||

### [8.20.29](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : prāṇeṣv indriyeṣu ||29||

### [8.20.30](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.30?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.20.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tad eva pratyakṣībhūya sudarśanādaya īśam upatasthur iti dvābhyām anvayaḥ | vidyādhara-saṁjño’siḥ | śata-candraṁ phalakaṁ tad-yuktaḥ ||31||

### [8.20.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : pārṣadeṣu mukhyāḥ sunanda-mukhyā iti teeṣv api sunando mukhya ity arthaḥ | kuṇḍala ity antaṁ pṛthak padam ||32||

### [8.20.33](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.33?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : madhu-vratānāṁ sraṅ mālākāraḥ samūho yatra tathā-bhūtayā vana-mālayā vṛto vyāptaḥ | urukramatvam evāha—kṣitim iti ||33||

### [8.20.34](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/20.34?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : dvitīyaṁ vāma-padaṁ kramamāṇasya tasya tripiṣṭapaṁ tadīyaṁ prāptam evābhūd iti śeṣaḥ |tṛtīyāya tṛtīya-pada-nyāsārtham aṇv api nāvaśiṣṭam ity arthaḥ | atra tripada-mātra-bhūmeḥ pratigṛhītatve’pi bhujādy-aṅgair nabha-ādi-vyāptiś ca nā’nyāyyā | tāvad bhūmāv ūrdhvāvasthiter apy apekṣitatvāditi sandarbhaḥ | nabha-ādīnām api pada-dvayāntar-bhūtatvāt pada-dvayenaiva vyāptir ucyata ity aparaiḥ ||34||

## Глава 21

### [8.21.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ānarca caraṇaṁ brahmā daityān yuddhān nyavārayat | baliṁ taṁ garuḍo’vadhnad ekaviṁśe’rtha-kovidaḥ || aṅghriḥ satya-lokaṁ gata ity uktaṁ, tataḥ kiṁ vṛttam ? ity ata āha—satyam iti | nakhā eva indavas taiḥ saha satyaṁ satya-lokaṁ samīkṣyābja-bhavo brahmā abhyagāt, marīcy-ādayaś cābhyaguḥ | vavandire abja-bhavaś ca vavande ity ubhayeṣām ubhayoru-bhaya-kriyānvayaḥ | kīdṛśaḥ ? nakhendubhir hatā tiraskṛtāḥ sva-dhāma-dyutayo yasya saḥ | svayaṁ ca tair āvṛtaś channa iti nakhendubhir ity asya triṣv apy anvayaḥ ||1||

### [8.21.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tarko nyāya-śāstram | itihāso bhāratādiḥ | aṅgāni śikṣādīni | purāṇāni brāhmādīni | saṁhitāḥ brahma-saṁhitāś ca ||2||

### [8.21.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : akarmakaṁ na vidyante karmakāṇi nikṛṣṭa-karmāṇi sādhanatvena yasya tat | abhyagṛṇāt tuṣṭāva ||3||

### [8.21.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kamaṇḍalu-jalaṁ pādāvanejanena pavitraṁ bhūtvā gaṅgābhūt | pañcama-skandhe tu sumeru-varṇane vāma-pādāṅguṣṭha-nakha-nirbhinnordhvāṇḍa-kaṭāha-bahir-jaladhāraiva gaṅgā | kvacit tu sākṣān nārāyaṇa eva drava-rūpeṇa gaṅgety ato jala-tritayam eva militaṁ gaṅgābhūt iti jñeyam | patatī patantī nimārṣṭi pavitrayati ||4||

### [8.21.5-7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : saṅkṣiptātma-vibhūtaye trivikrama-svarūpam antardhāpya vāmana-svarūpeṇaiva sthitāyety arthaḥ | tad eva brahmādayas tatraivāgatya toyādibhiḥ pādyādibhir baliṁ pūjām ājahrur upakalpayāmāsuḥ ||5||

### [8.21.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.21.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.21.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : barhiṣi yajñe ||11||

### [8.21.12-15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.21.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : jaya-vijaya iti bhagavato brahmaṇyatvasya bhaktānām aparādha-vibhīṣikāyāś ca pradarśanārtham evānayoḥ prakāśāv eva vaikuṇṭhād adhaḥ petatur iti tṛtīye vaikuṇṭha-varṇana eva vyākhyātam | nāgā hastinaḥ, te ca lokālokopari-vartino bhagavad-vibhūti-rūpā vaikuṇṭha-vartino vā jñeyāḥ ||16-17||

### [8.21.19](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.19?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.21.20](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.20?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.21.21](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.21?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.21.22](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.22?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : kālaṁ kāla-rūpiṇaṁ prabhum ||22||

### [8.21.23](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.23?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : evaṁ tattvopadeśam agṛhṇatas tamo-grastān surān ālakṣya protsāhanena daityānukūlam atropadeśena nivartayitum āha—bhavadbhir iti | daivena ṛddhaiḥ samṛddhair bhavadbhiḥ ||23||

### [8.21.24](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.24?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : arthatvāyārtha-sādhakatvāya ||24||

### [8.21.25](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.25?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : rasāṁ rasātalam ||25||

### [8.21.26](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.26?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : viṣu pakṣiṣu rājata iti virāṭ | prabhoś cikīrṣitaṁ jñātveti | asya mamatāspadaṁ sarvam aṅgīkṛtya ahantāspadam apy aṅgīkartum icchati, mat-prabhuḥ pratidānāsāmarthyam abhidyotāsya ṛṇī bhavan dvārapālo bubhūṣati | svasya bhaktādhīnatvaṁ bhaktasya ca sarvotkarṣaṁ lokeṣu khyāpayitum ato daṇḍenāpy anaśvaram asya dhairyaṁ darśayāmi sarva-lokān iti babandha | sūtye’hani somābhiṣava-dine ||26||

### [8.21.27](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.27?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : rodasyor dyāv-āpṛthivyoḥ ||27||

### [8.21.28](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.28?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bhraṣṭa-śriyaṁ vigata-sampatkam | tad api sthira-prajñam akṣubdha-dhiyam | yata udāra-yaśasaṁ sampatter apacaye’pi yaśaso’tyupacayaṁ prāptam ity arthaḥ ||28||

### [8.21.29](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.29?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : mahī tvat-svāmikaṁ sthānam ||29||

### [8.21.30](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.30?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : asau sūryaḥ ||30||

### [8.21.31](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.31?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tanos tanvā | svaṁ tadīyaṁ dhanam | ātmanā svarūpeṇaiva na ca trivikramatvam anyadīyaṁ svarūpam iti bhāvaḥ | padaikena mayākrānta iti pūrvato lokālokāt paścimato lokāloka ekenaiva padā mayākrāntaḥ ity arthaḥ ||31||

### [8.21.32](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.32?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tarhi mayā kiṁ kartavyam ? iti cet, niraye vāsaḥ kriyatām iti tasya dhīratvaṁ nirupādhi-bhakti-niṣṭhāṁ ca loke khyāpayitum prakaṭam āha—pratīti | niraye narake guruṇā anumodita iti kim ahaṁ yuktaṁ bravīmy ayuktaṁ vā ? iti tattvaṁ sva-guruṁ vidvāṁsaṁ śukrācāryam eva pṛṣṭveti bhāvaḥ | vastutas tu bhaktābhāsasyāpi narakāsambhavāt, pratiśrutam adātur api te niraye ralayor aikyāt nilaye madīye vaikuṇṭhe eva vāsa iṣyate ucito bhavati | yadyapi tadapi samprati nilayaṁ mayā dīyamānaṁ sutalākhyaṁ madīya-sthāna-viśeṣaṁ viśa, tvad-adhikārānte eva vaikuṇṭhe tvāṁ svāsayiṣyāmīti bhāvaḥ | guruṇā śukreṇeti yadyapi tvaṁ sva-guruṁ taṁ mad-vimukhaṁ jñātvā sampraty avamanyase, tad api sa tvad-viṣayaka-sneha-bhareṇaiva lupta-viveko mamātipriya eva mayāvagataḥ | atas tena sahaiva viśety arthaḥ ||32||

### [8.21.33](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.33?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : vṛtheti prakaṭārthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ | agrima-bhagavad-vākya-tat-phala-dṛṣṭyā vastv-arthaś caiva vyākhyāyate, tasya prasiddha-mad-bhaktasya bhavata indra-padam adhyupaṣyāsam iti manoratho vṛthaiva yatas tatto vaikuṇṭha-sthāt sakāśāt dūraḥ svargo’dhaḥ patati | sampraty api bhavān sutalaṁ prasthāpyamāno’pi svargād ūrdhvam adhirohasi, sutala-bhogasya svarge’py avidyamānatvād iti bhāvaḥ | yo bhavān sva-dehātma-sarvasva-samarpaṇāt pratiśrutasya ā samyak prakārato dānena mām arthinaṁ puruṣārtha-catuṣṭayavantam api viśeṣataḥ prakarṣeṇa labhate, tvadīya-dvārapālo bhūtvā sthāsyāmīti bhāvaḥ ||33||

### [8.21.34](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/21.34?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : tataḥ kim iti cet śrūyatām ity āha—vipralabdha iti | āḍhyebhyaḥ śakrādibhyo’pi māninā lokair dīyamāna-saṁmānavatā tvayāhaṁ yato viśeṣataḥ prakarṣeṇa labdhaḥ, tat tasmāt iti dadāmi | vyalīkaṁ vigatālīkaṁ mayā dīyamānatvāt parama-satyaṁ phalaṁ yatra tat nilayaṁ sutala-sambandhi sampadaṁ katicit samāḥ saṁvatsarān vyāpya bhuṅkṣva tato’ṣṭame manvantare tvām indra-padaṁ prāpayya sva-dhāma vaikuṇṭham eva neṣye iti bhāvaḥ ||34||

## Глава 22

### [8.22.1](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.1?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : bali-prahlādayor vindyāvali-padmajayor api | sūktir varān adād asmai dvāviṁśe varuṇāmbudhiḥ || babandha kapaṭī yasmād baliṁ niragham īśvaraḥ | tatas tad-dvāri tat-prema-paśair baddhaḥ sadāvasat || evam anena prakāreṇa viprakṛto’pakṛtaḥ bhidyamaṇaḥ sattvāc cālyamānaḥ ||1||

### [8.22.2](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.2?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : uttama-śloketi sva-prabhau sa-narmoktiḥ | svasya vāmanasya tribhiḥ padaiḥ parimitāṁ bhūmiṁ bhikṣitvā, svarūpāntarasya trivikramasya tribhiḥ padaiḥ pratigrahītuṁ prayatamānasya nirlobhasya vipra-baṭos tava nispṛhatvaṁ vyaktībhūtam ity etāṁ kīrti-sudhām eva nirapāyāṁ pāyaṁ pāyam eva bhaktā vayam ānanda-mattā bhavāma, yad aho lakṣmī-kānto’pi māṁ tāvad atiraṅkam api bhūmiṁ bhikṣase | tatrāpi chalenādhika-jighṛkṣā | tatrāpi baṭu-veśatvena baṭu-janānāṁ kapaṭam artha-spṛhām aśāntim aprāptyā kopaṁ dātari daṇḍaṁ ca svābhāvikaṁ dharmam abhivyajya teṣāṁ viḍambanam | śāstrābhijñānāṁ mad-gurūṇām api buddhi-lopaḥ | sva-kṛpā-paramāṇu-mātra-spṛṣṭeṣv indrādiṣu sva-pakṣatva-vyañjanā | sva-kṛpāmṛta-mahodadhi-madhya-magne mayi vipakṣatva-vyañjanety evam ādāya eva tavottamāḥ ślokāḥ kavibhiḥ ślokair gāsyanta iti bhāvaḥ | yadi bhavān mad-vaco vyalīkaṁ mithyā manyate iti sva-bhaktam anyāyenāpi jigīṣuṇā tvayā, tat sva-bhakta-vaco mithyā kartum aśakyam eveti bhāvaḥ | he sura-varya ! surair varaṇīya ! trailokyaṁ bhikṣitvā baleḥ sakāśād ānīya asmabhyaṁ bhogārthaṁ dehīti surair manye varaṁ prārthito bhavān abhūd iti bhāvaḥ | tad-vaca ṛtaṁ satyaṁ karomi | mad-uktaṁ hi pralambhanaṁ na bhavet | yathā sva-caraṇa-premāmṛtam adadānasya suraiḥ stutasya tava vacas trivarga-mātra-pradāyakatvāt sura-pralambhakam iti bhāvaḥ | me śīrṣṇi nijaṁ tṛtīyaṁ padaṁ kuru | na ca « dvābhyāṁ viśvaṁ krāntavato mama tava śiraḥ pāda-paryāptaṁ na bhavati » iti manyethāḥ | vittena cet pada-dvayaṁ jātaṁ, tarhīdam adhikam eva syāt | vittād api vitta-svāmino’dhikatvād iti bhāvaḥ ||2||

### [8.22.3](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.3?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu kiṁ vāruṇa-pāśa-bandha-bhayāt naraka-bhayād vā ātmānaṁ dātum icchasi ? iti, tatrāha—bibhemīti | artha-kṛcchrāt dravyopārjana-kaṣṭāt | asādhu-vādād bhagavad-bhaktā brāhmaṇa-vañcakā bhavanti yathā balir iti vaiṣṇava-loka-duṣkīrti-vādāt ||3||

### [8.22.4](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.4?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu mayā nigrahāt tava duṣkīrtir jāyate vā ? iti, tatrāha—puṁsām iti | hitaiṣitvāt daṇḍayanto’pi mātrādayo yaṁ daṇḍam ā samyak prakāreṇa na diśanti na dadati, mātrādayo aihika-hitaiṣiṇo’rhattamās tu pāralaukika-hitaiṣiṇo mātṛ-koṭibhyo’py ativatsalā iti bhāvaḥ ||4||

### [8.22.5](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.5?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanv ahaṁ devānāṁ hitaiṣī prasiddho, nāsurāṇām ? tatrāha—tvam iti | guru-hita-kārī parokṣaḥ | śatru-cchalena vartamānatvād iti bhāvaḥ | pratyakṣa-hita-kāritvād api parokṣa-hita-kāritvaṁ pratyadhika-sūcakam, ata eva paramo devānām antv aparamaḥ, tat-kāmitaiśvarya-pradattena vāstava-hitaiṣitvābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ | asmākaṁ tu tvaṁ vāstva-hita-kṛd evety āha—ya iti cakṣur iti devānāṁ tv ādhyām iti bhāvaḥ | ādiśad iti prathama-puruṣa ārṣaḥ ||5||

### [8.22.6-7](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.6?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : mādṛśānāṁ tvad-ekānta-bhaktānāṁ khalu kā vārtā ? ye punar asurās tvayi vairam anubadhnanti, teṣv api tava tāvad alaukiky eva dayā, ity āha—yasminn iti | tena guruṇā bhūri-karmaṇeti man-nigrahas te bahu-kāryathārthaḥ | tathā hi—kiñcin-mātrī me vrīḍā utpadyate sā khalu me citta-śuddhy-abhāvād eveti bhāvaḥ ||6-7||

### [8.22.8](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.8?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : asmat-kula-daivatatvenaiva tvad-daṇḍo mamāvaśyaḥ sahya eva tvam api mad-bhakta-pautro’yam iti buddhyaiva mayi snihyasīty āha—pitāmaha iti | bhavad-vipakṣeṇa hiraṇyakaśipuṇā vicitraṁ vipattiṁ prāpitaḥ prāpito’pi ||8||

### [8.22.9](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.9?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : nanu so’pi pitaram anupekṣya kim iti māṁ prapede, tatrāha—kim iti dvābhyām | ātmanā dehena | rikthaṁ tvat-sevārthakam api dhanaṁ harantīti taiḥ ||9||

### [8.22.10](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.10?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : sva-pakṣaṁ daitya-kulaṁ kṣapayatīti tasya ||10||

### [8.22.11](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.11?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : ātma-ripor iti vyāja-stutyaivoktiḥ | vastutas tu ātmanaḥ parama-priya-suhṛdaḥ | yad vā, ātmanaḥ sthūla-sūkṣma-deha-dvayasya ripor nāśakasya mokṣa-pradasya ity arthaḥ | yad vā, ātmano mad-ahaṅkārasya śatros tvayādya mama tribhuvanādhīśatvāhaṅkāra-mahā-rogaḥ sādhu nāśita ity arthaḥ | daivena prahlāda-pautratva-prāpakena bhāgyena yayā śriyā dhvasta-buddhir ayaṁ mal-lakṣaṇo jana idaṁ jīvitam adhruvaṁ na budhyata ity ato dhanvantarim iva mat-sarva-roga-cikitsakaṁ tvām ahaṁ bhāgyena prāpta iti bhāvaḥ ||11||

### [8.22.12](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.12?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : na vyākhyātam.

### [8.22.13](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.13?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : subhaga-rṣabhaṁ sarva-loka-priyam ||13||

### [8.22.14](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.14?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ : aparādhaṁ vinā kathaṁ bandhanam ity aparādha-lakṣaṇasya prahlāda-dṛṣṭatvāt sa-vrīḍam | yad vā, prahlādāt sadaiva śikṣitasya nirabhimānatva-lakṣaṇasya dharmasya bhūmi-dāna-prastāve sahasā vismaraṇāt tad-darśane sati sa-vrīḍam | sa-vrīḍatvād eva nīcīnaṁ mukhaṁ yasya saḥ ||14||

### [8.22.15](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.15?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :na vyākhyātam.

### [8.22.16](https://vedarama.ru/st/books/sb8-5comm/22.16?reading=1)

viśvanāthaḥ :tvayaiveti | na hy aindraṁ padam etadīyaṁ tvayāpahṛtaṁ, kintu svīyam eva punaḥ svīkṛtaṁ, tac ca śobhanam eva kṛtam | yataḥ saṁyato’pi janaḥ, tat tasyāṁ sampadi satyāṁ ka ātmano gatis tattvaṁ yathāvad vicaṣṭe ? no ko’pīty arthaḥ | na ca atra tava dattāpahāra-lakṣaṇo doṣo’pi snehena putra-haste dattasyāpi modakāder ahitāśaṅkayā punar acchidya nītavataḥ pitur yathā tathety arthaḥ ||16||
